### former-hokkaido-government-office — verified_walking

- Canonical arrival: Sapporo Station (repository bus endpoint)
- Origin-travel coverage: Origin-travel coverage was evaluated against Sapporo Station (repository bus endpoint); no unrecorded hub or destination parent was substituted.
- Required local legs: The official access topology and any required final segments were reviewed; unresolved segments are not silently omitted.
- Walking / paid assessment: A verified walking fact was not selected unless an authoritative source tied the exact physical arrival to a practical walk. / Paid access was retained only when a current saleable route/product and exact coverage were established; otherwise it remains unresolved.
- Fare/product and coverage: No fare/product is fabricated; any fare gap is explicitly separated from retrieval failures.; No numeric coverage is asserted for an unresolved fact; topology-blocked cases remain unavailable only because the physical origin context is absent.
- Multiple segments: Multiple required segments were considered; no through-fare or segment coverage is assumed without source evidence.
- No double counting: Origin-travel legs, destination-local legs, optional admission, and alternative routes remain separate; no parent ID is used as an arrival point.
- Sources: http://www.sapporo.travel/spot/facility/akarenga/

### sapporo-clock-tower — verified_walking

- Canonical arrival: Sapporo Station (repository bus endpoint)
- Origin-travel coverage: Origin-travel coverage was evaluated against Sapporo Station (repository bus endpoint); no unrecorded hub or destination parent was substituted.
- Required local legs: The official access topology and any required final segments were reviewed; unresolved segments are not silently omitted.
- Walking / paid assessment: A verified walking fact was not selected unless an authoritative source tied the exact physical arrival to a practical walk. / Paid access was retained only when a current saleable route/product and exact coverage were established; otherwise it remains unresolved.
- Fare/product and coverage: No fare/product is fabricated; any fare gap is explicitly separated from retrieval failures.; No numeric coverage is asserted for an unresolved fact; topology-blocked cases remain unavailable only because the physical origin context is absent.
- Multiple segments: Multiple required segments were considered; no through-fare or segment coverage is assumed without source evidence.
- No double counting: Origin-travel legs, destination-local legs, optional admission, and alternative routes remain separate; no parent ID is used as an arrival point.
- Sources: https://sapporoshi-tokeidai.jp/access/

### tsukiji-outer-market — verified_required_access

- Canonical arrival: Tokyo Station (Tokyo:chuo repository Shinkansen/bus endpoint) → Tokyo Metro Tsukiji Station
- Origin-travel coverage: Origin-travel coverage was evaluated against Tokyo Station (Tokyo:chuo repository Shinkansen/bus endpoint) → Tokyo Metro Tsukiji Station; no unrecorded hub or destination parent was substituted.
- Required local legs: The official access topology and any required final segments were reviewed; unresolved segments are not silently omitted.
- Walking / paid assessment: A verified walking fact was not selected unless an authoritative source tied the exact physical arrival to a practical walk. / Paid access was retained only when a current saleable route/product and exact coverage were established; otherwise it remains unresolved.
- Fare/product and coverage: No fare/product is fabricated; any fare gap is explicitly separated from retrieval failures.; No numeric coverage is asserted for an unresolved fact; topology-blocked cases remain unavailable only because the physical origin context is absent.
- Multiple segments: Multiple required segments were considered; no through-fare or segment coverage is assumed without source evidence.
- No double counting: Origin-travel legs, destination-local legs, optional admission, and alternative routes remain separate; no parent ID is used as an arrival point.
- Sources: https://www.tsukiji.or.jp/english/access/, https://transfer.tokyometro.jp/?lang=en
